# Agents · S · part 8

1,030 entries, sd-reviewer to SDK_PROJECT_HISTORY_ERASURE. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sd-reviewer**: Opus-class agent that reviews pull requests for correctness, design, and long-term maintainability. Distinguishes blocking from nit, leaves specific and kind feedback, and gives an explicit approve / request-changes verdict. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/blob/HEAD/examples/cartridges/software-development/agents/sd-reviewer.md) · ★71)
- **sd-reviewer**: Severity-tagged compliance review. Five task types covering holistic, standalone, bug-fix-final, perf-final, and port-parity review. Every finding cites file:line and a constitution §section. Never auto-fixes, never prescribes exact code. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md) · ★10)
- **sd-reviewer**: Code reviewer for sessiondeck work units. Reads a diff range and the phase requirements it claims to satisfy, verifies claims by reading code and running tests, and returns ranked findings with concrete failure scenarios. Read-only; never edits. Use after every executor unit. ([stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck](https://github.com/stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sd-reviewer.md))
- **sd-sandbox**: Front-end developer for the SMART Disburse onboarding sandbox (src/pages/onboarding, src/scripts/onboarding-*). Implements and fixes pages so they match the Figma design at phone, tablet and desktop, without regressions. Use for any change that must happen in the repo code. Writes only repo code; reads Figma for reference. SMART Disburse ONLY (src/pages/onboarding/, src/scripts/onboarding-* and the SD scripts, Figma page Multi-Party Flow of [LAUNCHED] Default Flow). For other SMART products use the suite agents (proto-engineer, figma-designer, ui-qa-code, ui-qa-figma), not this one. ([vasileschidu/test-sx](https://github.com/vasileschidu/test-sx/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sd-sandbox.md) · ★1)
- **sd-security-reviewer**: Security reviewer for sessiondeck. Gate for fixtures (S0b), settings merge and token handling (S1), hub auth and input validation (S3), transcript redaction (S7), box hardening and Cloudflare edge (S11, S12), and the runner (S13). Threat-models the unit, verifies controls by reading code and running read-only checks, returns findings with exploit scenarios. Read-only. ([stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck](https://github.com/stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sd-security-reviewer.md))
- **sd-slice-builder**: Implementation worker for a planned slice of multi-file code work — code plus its tests and fail-first evidence, in its own worktree, ending in local commits merged with origin/main, a pull-request body file and a report. The lead reviews, tests and enqueues; the lane gates and pushes. Use for any slice, work item or fix that writes code across several files and runs longer than a few minutes. Not for lookups that change no file, single-claim checks or one-file edits. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/sd-slice-builder.md) · ★1)
- **sd-source-reader**: Bounded read-only worker that consumes one source against an extraction brief and returns a structured, provenance-tagged extract for its parent to synthesize. ([platypeeps/sd-workflow](https://github.com/platypeeps/sd-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/sd-source-reader.md) · ★1)
- **sd-spec-architect**: Creates, refines, and plans specs across all 6 workflow types (feature, bug, refactor, perf, rca, port). Reads CLAUDE.md and constitution.md at runtime. Use this agent for any spec authoring or atomic-task planning. ([developzoneio/specwright](https://github.com/developzoneio/specwright/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-architect.md) · ★10)
- **sd-sprint-orchestrator**: Mini-sprint orchestrator for sessiondeck, one per sprint, headless inside its own container. Creates the sprint branch, launches phase-manager sessions in worktrees per the package in MINI-SPRINT-ORDER.md, monitors their status files, relaunches blocked sessions when answers arrive, merges green branches in order, runs the integration gate with test containers, opens the PR to main, and stops at awaiting-merge-approval. Never writes code, never merges to main. Use with the level-1 prompt from PROMPT-BOOK.md section 3. ([stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck](https://github.com/stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sd-sprint-orchestrator.md))
- **sd-tech-lead**: Opus-class agent that owns technical direction for the cartridge. Makes design and architecture decisions, routes work to the other sd agents, arbitrates review disagreements, and acts as capcom. The single technical voice that approves merging to main and cutting releases. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/blob/HEAD/examples/cartridges/software-development/agents/sd-tech-lead.md) · ★71)
- **sd-test-engineer**: Sonnet agent that owns the test suite — writes new unit and integration tests, keeps the suite fast and deterministic, hunts flaky tests, and enforces the rule that every bug fix ships with a failing-then-passing regression test. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/blob/HEAD/examples/cartridges/software-development/agents/sd-test-engineer.md) · ★71)
- **sd-verifier**: Verifies sessiondeck requirements with evidence. Given requirement IDs and a phase doc, runs the tests, benches, and harnesses that prove each one, and writes docs/VERIFY-P&lt;phase&gt;.md (and docs/*-BENCH.md when a bench is involved) with command, output, and pass/fail per requirement. Does not fix anything. Use before any task is marked complete and at the end of every session. ([stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck](https://github.com/stack-dev-personal/sessiondeck/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sd-verifier.md))
- **sd-web-agents**: Anything the next agent picking up this lane should know. ([dmarzzz/swarm-dynamics-lab](https://github.com/dmarzzz/swarm-dynamics-lab/blob/HEAD/lab/researchers/dmarz/agents/sd-web-agents.md) · ★3)

- **sdcd-reviewer**: Post-commit or pre-commit code review against SDCD rules and the active plan. Use after a substantial change (new feature, new module, significant refactor) to catch scope creep, §1 violations, stale brain files, and spec drift before pushing. The solo-dev's stand-in reviewer. ([Tschonsen/spec-driven-claude-development](https://github.com/Tschonsen/spec-driven-claude-development/blob/HEAD/plugin/sdcd/agents/sdcd-reviewer.md))
- **sdd**: You are an expert in **Spec-Driven Development**, a workflow that transforms vague ideas into robust, well-tested software through a systematic, multi-phase approach. Your role is to guide users through the complete SDD workflow by orchestrating specialized subagents. ([josephschmitt/dotfiles](https://github.com/josephschmitt/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/personal/.config/opencode/agents/sdd.md) · ★13)
- **sdd**: Stapler-Driven Development (SDD) coordinator agent. Runs the full 7-phase development workflow — ideate, research, plan, validate, implement, verify, ship — or any subset of phases. Use when you need to take a feature from idea to shipped PR with architecture review, adversarial planning, and test-first implementation. Invoke with a feature description and optionally specify which phases to run (e.g. "planning only" for phases 1–4, "implement" to start from phase 5). ([tstapler/dotfiles](https://github.com/tstapler/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sdd.md) · ★8)
- **sdd**: Agente SDD: direct (1–3), SDD-Tiny (4–8) y SDD-Lite (9+) son recomendaciones; standard es legacy por solicitud explícita. Quick Plan solo para SDD-Lite; conserva gates y pruebas. ([furthurr/ai-agents-kit](https://github.com/furthurr/ai-agents-kit/blob/HEAD/generated/pi/agents/sdd.md) · ★3)

Carga `data-api` o `ui-design` bajo demanda como conoc ([furthurr/ai-agents-kit](https://github.com/furthurr/ai-agents-kit/blob/HEAD/canonical/agents/sdd.md) · ★3)
- **sdd**: Aplica SDD proporcional: direct (1–3), SDD-Tiny (4–8) y SDD-Lite (9+); standard legacy por solicitud explícita. Quick Plan solo para SDD-Lite; conserva gates, elección y pruebas. ([furthurr/ai-agents-kit](https://github.com/furthurr/ai-agents-kit/blob/HEAD/generated/antigravity/agents/sdd.md) · ★3)
- **sdd**: Orchestrator of the Spec-Driven Development (SDD) flow. Use me when you are going to implement a spec. I coordinate Explorer, Planner, Crit, Implementer, Reviewer, test-writer and code-reviewer in the right order. ([deus-magna/zero-code-full-flutter](https://github.com/deus-magna/zero-code-full-flutter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/sdd.md) · ★1)
- **sdd**: **Description:** Development methodology where the specification (*spec*) is the primary artifact and source of truth of the project. Instead of writing code directly, you define *what* the system should do in structured natural language, and an AI agent translates that specification into implementation. The spec is not accessory documentation — it is the *input* that governs the entire cycle: cod ([dramirezbe/RAG-study](https://github.com/dramirezbe/RAG-study/blob/HEAD/agents/sdd.md))
- **sdd**: Agente de planejamento. Use antes de implementar uma feature ou bug não trivial — recebe o pedido, tira ambiguidade e escreve spec.md + tasks.md em .docs/features/&lt;slug&gt;/ ou .docs/bugs/&lt;slug&gt;/. - **sdk-complexity-devil-go**: Testing-phase agent (in T5 cohort). Runs scaling benchmarks at N ∈ {10, 100, 1k, 10k} for any §7 symbol that takes variable-size input. Fits the curve. Compares measured complexity to design/perf-budget.md's declared big-O. BLOCKER on mismatch (e.g. declared O(n) measured O(n²)). Prevents an accidental quadratic path from passing because the microbenchmark only ran at N=100. READ-ONLY. Backs G107. ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-complexity-devil-go.md))
- **sdk-complexity-devil-python**: Wave T5 testing-phase devil. Reads perf-budget.md complexity.time declarations, runs scaling sweep at N ∈ {10, 100, 1k, 10k} via pytest-benchmark + parametrize for any §7 symbol that takes variable-size input, curve-fits log-log slope, compares to declared big-O. BLOCKER on mismatch. Catches the accidental quadratic that micro-benches at fixed N never expose. Backs G107-py (perf-confidence axis 4). ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-complexity-devil-python.md))
- **sdk-constraint-devil**: Verifies [constraint:] invariants survive every proposed change. READ-ONLY except for running benchmarks. - **sdk-drift-detector**: Testing-phase agent (T-SOAK wave). Observes soak state files on a poll ladder (30s, 2m, 5m, 15m, 30m, 60m, 2h, 4h, 6h). Fits linear regression over time on drift_signals (heap_bytes, goroutines, gc_pause_p99_ns, etc.). Fast-fails on statistically significant positive slope (p&lt;0.05). Issues PASS / FAIL / INCOMPLETE per MMD. Backs G105 (MMD) + G106 (drift) + rule 33. READ-ONLY. ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-drift-detector.md))

- **sdk-skill-synthesizer**: Phase -1 Wave B2. Drafts missing skill SKILL.md files per SKILL-CREATION-GUIDE. May invoke Context7/Exa for canonical sources. - **sdk-soak-runner-go**: Testing-phase agent (T-SOAK wave). Spawns soak tests via Bash run_in_background so they outlive Claude's tool-call window. Writes structured state file (ops, heap_bytes, goroutines, gc_pause_p99_ns, status, drift_signals) every N ops / 30 s. Produces verdict PASS / FAIL / INCOMPLETE per design/perf-budget.md MMD. Backs G105 (soak MMD) + rule 33 (Verdict Taxonomy). ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-soak-runner-go.md))
- **sdk-soak-runner-python**: Wave T5.5 testing-phase agent. Spawns long-running Python soak tests via Bash run_in_background so they outlive the 10-minute tool-call window. Authors a per-symbol soak harness; the harness runs the bench in a loop at declared concurrency and writes JSONL drift snapshots (rss_bytes, tracemalloc_top_size_bytes, asyncio_pending_tasks, gc_count_gen2, open_fds, thread_count, plus per-symbol drift_signals from perf-budget.md) every 30 s. Hands off the observe phase to sdk-drift-detector. Backs G105-py (soak MMD), G106-py (drift), CLAUDE.md rule 33 (Verdict Taxonomy). ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-soak-runner-python.md))

- **sdk-tester**: Use this agent to verify SDK behavioral claims by running E2E scripts against a local Midnight devnet. Checks devnet health first, then writes raw SDK scripts or testkit-js tests to exercise the full transaction pipeline. Dispatched by the /midnight-verify:verify command. Example 1: Claim "deployContract deploys and returns a contract address" — writes a raw SDK script that deploys a counter contract, checks the result has a contractAddress field with a valid hex string. Example 2: Claim "full deploy+call+observe lifecycle works" — uses testkit-js to set up environment, deploy, call increment… ([midnightntwrk/midnight-expert](https://github.com/midnightntwrk/midnight-expert/blob/HEAD/plugins/midnight-verify/agents/sdk-tester.md) · ★47)

- **sdk-upgrade**: Upgrades the @gusto/embedded-react-sdk dependency in this demo app and migrates the routed demos to match. Researches release notes for the target version, classifies each change as ship-ready, work-in-progress, or contractor (skip), implements the ship-ready changes following the build-domain-demo conventions, verifies the build, and returns a structured report. Use when bumping the SDK version, a new SDK version is published, or migrating SDK block components. Runs standalone (no PR or review-request side effects); the update-embedded-react-sdk skill wraps it for CI. ([Gusto/embedded-react-sdk-demo-app](https://github.com/Gusto/embedded-react-sdk-demo-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sdk-upgrade.md) · ★1)
